00294: def mesh_descendants(root):
00295:     if root.type == 'MESH':
00296:         yield root
00297:     for child in root.children:
00298:         yield from mesh_descendants(child)
00299: 
00300: for obj in bpy.data.objects:
00301:     if obj.type != 'MESH':
00302:         continue
00303:     for material in obj.data.materials:
00304:         if material is None:
00305:             continue
00306:         material.use_nodes = True
00307:         shader = material.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')

00196: def make_material(name,color,roughness=.5,metallic=0):
00197:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00198:     mat.diffuse_color = (*color,1)
00199:     mat.use_nodes = True
00200:     shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00201:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00202:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00203:     shader.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00204:     return mat
00205: 
00206: def make_mesh(name,vertices,faces,material,parent_name):
00207:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00208:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices,[],faces)
00209:     mesh.update()
00210:     obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00211:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00212:     obj.data.materials.append(material)
00213:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00214:     if parent is not None:
00215:         obj.parent = parent
00216:         obj.matrix_parent_inverse = parent.matrix_world.inverted()
00217:     return obj

00314: def make_material(name, color, roughness=.5, metallic=0):
00315:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00316:     mat.diffuse_color = (*color, 1)
00317:     mat.use_nodes = True
00318:     shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00319:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color, 1)
00320:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00321:     shader.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00322:     return mat
00323: 
00324: def make_mesh(name, vertices, faces, material, parent_name):
00325:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00326:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices, [], faces)
00327:     mesh.update()
00328:     obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)
00329:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00330:     obj.data.materials.append(material)
00331:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00332:     if parent is not None:
00333:         obj.parent = parent
00334:         obj.matrix_parent_inverse = parent.matrix_world.inverted()
00335:     return obj

00364:         c = math.cos(angle)
00365:         s = math.sin(angle)
00366:         vertices.append((
00367:             .32 + rx*math.copysign(abs(c)**.72, c),
00368:             -.19 + ry*math.copysign(abs(s)**.72, s),

00438: # Traverse asset descendants when assigning material variations.
00439: def mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00440:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(marker_name)
00441:     if marker is None:
00442:         return []
00443:     result = []
00444:     pending = [marker]
00445:     while pending:
00446:         obj = pending.pop()
00447:         if obj.type == "MESH":
00448:             result.append(obj)
00449:         pending.extend(list(obj.children))
00450:     return result
00451: 
00452: def customize_asset_materials(marker_name, callback):
00453:     for obj in mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00454:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00455:             if slot.material is None:
00456:                 continue
00457:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00458:             slot.material = mat
00459:             mat.use_nodes = True
00460:             shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00461:             if shader is not None:
00462:                 callback(mat, shader)
00463:

00476:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.57, .52, .39, 1)

00192: def make_material(name,color,roughness=.5,metallic=0):
00193:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00194:     mat.diffuse_color = (*color,1)
00195:     mat.use_nodes = True
00196:     shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00197:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00198:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00199:     shader.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00200:     return mat

00202: def make_mesh(name,vertices,faces,material,parent_name):
00203:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00204:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices,[],faces)
00205:     mesh.update()
00206:     obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00207:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00208:     obj.data.materials.append(material)
00209:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00210:     if parent is not None:
00211:         obj.parent = parent
00212:         obj.matrix_parent_inverse = parent.matrix_world.inverted()
00213:     return obj

00284: def mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00285:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(marker_name)
00286:     if marker is None:
00287:         return []
00288:     result,pending = [],[marker]
00289:     while pending:
00290:         obj = pending.pop()
00291:         if obj.type == "MESH":
00292:             result.append(obj)
00293:         pending.extend(list(obj.children))
00294:     return result
00295: 
00296: def customize_asset_materials(marker_name,callback):
00297:     for obj in mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00298:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00299:             if slot.material is None:
00300:                 continue
00301:             mat = slot.material.copy()

00303:             mat.use_nodes = True
00304:             shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00305:             if shader is not None:
00306:                 callback(mat,shader)

00196: def make_material(name,color,roughness=.5,metallic=0):
00197:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00198:     mat.diffuse_color = (*color,1)
00199:     mat.use_nodes = True
00200:     shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00201:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00202:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00203:     shader.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00204:     return mat
00205: 
00206: def make_mesh(name,vertices,faces,material,parent_name):
00207:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00208:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices,[],faces)
00209:     mesh.update()
00210:     obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00211:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00212:     obj.data.materials.append(material)
00213:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00214:     if parent is not None:
00215:         obj.parent = parent
00216:         obj.matrix_parent_inverse = parent.matrix_world.inverted()
00217:     return obj
00218: 
00219: # Customize existing materials by their explicit source colors.
00220: for mat in list(bpy.data.materials):
00221:     mat.use_nodes = True
00222:     shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00223:     if shader is None:
00224:         continue

00192: def material_for(color):
00193:     key = tuple(color)
00194:     if key not in material_cache:

00200:         shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .55
00201:         material_cache[key] = mat
00202:     return material_cache[key]

00206:         continue

00208:     lo = Vector(tuple(min(v[k] for v in corners) for k in range(3)))
00209:     hi = Vector(tuple(max(v[k] for v in corners) for k in range(3)))
00210:     center = (lo+hi)/2
00211:     size = hi-lo
00212:     best = None
00213:     best_error = 1e9
00214:     for rec in box_finishes:
00215:         c,s,col,name = rec
00216:         error = sum(abs(center[k]-c[k])+abs(size[k]-s[k]) for k in range(3))
00217:         if error < best_error:
00218:             best_error = error
00219:             best = rec
00220:     if best is not None and best_error < .025:

00185: # Preserve SceneAct's material color when enabling node shading.
00186: # Enabling nodes alone can otherwise leave the default pale shader.
00187: for mat in list(bpy.data.materials):

00189:     previously_used_nodes = mat.use_nodes
00190:     mat.use_nodes = True
00191:     shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00192:     if shader:
00193:         if not previously_used_nodes:
00194:             shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = base_color

00197: def meshes_below(obj):
00198:     if obj.type == "MESH":
00199:         yield obj
00200:     for child in obj.children:
00201:         yield from meshes_below(child)
